# Question event-set mapping audit

Read-only audit of the connected Evaluation Management Tool database on October 8, 2026. No question records or mappings were changed.

Of 1390 active questions, 940 lack an approved event-set mapping for at least one applicable active rating, 62 have approved mappings for all their applicable active ratings, and 388 have no active rating assignment.

A valid mapping is approved, targets an active event set in the shared eight-event-set sequence, and is unscoped or applies to an issuance sharing the same certificate/category/class/rating. Trigger selection is not required to assess mapping. A draft mapping is reported as Needs approval; it should be reviewed rather than duplicated. A question can appear under several ratings but appears once in the detailed list below.
